Response L4D1HealThanksNamVet
{
	scene "scenes/NamVet/Thanks04.vcd" predelay ".2,.5" then Subject YouWelcome foo:0 -0.724 //Thanks.
	scene "scenes/NamVet/Thanks05.vcd" predelay ".2,.5" then Subject YouWelcome foo:0 -0.915 //Thank you.
	scene "scenes/NamVet/Thanks06.vcd" predelay ".2,.5" then Subject YouWelcome foo:0 -1.257 //Thanks a lot.
	scene "scenes/NamVet/Thanks07.vcd" predelay ".2,.5" //I owe ya one.
	scene "scenes/NamVet/Thanks08.vcd" predelay ".2,.5" //That's one I owe you.
	scene "scenes/NamVet/Thanks11.vcd" predelay ".2,.5" //That wasn't necessary, but thanks.
}

{
	criteria ConceptHealedByFriend IsNotCoughing  IsNamVet IsTalk IsTalkNamVet SubjectIsNotNamVet IsWorldTalkNamVet
	ApplyContext "TalkNamVet:1:2"
	Response L4D1HealThanksNamVet
}
